**Summary**: Identified top 5 YouTube channels for code-graph + RAG 2026 with full 9Q multi-angle analysis per YOUTUBE-RESEARCH-GUIDE.

**Top 5 channels**:
1. **IBM Technology** — best conceptual explainers (GraphRAG vs Traditional RAG, 179K views)
2. **AI Engineer** — deepest interviews (Emil Eifrem/Neo4j CEO, GraphRAG marriage, 267K views)
3. **Cole Medin** — best practical builder content (RAG 2.0 + Knowledge Graphs template, 164K views)
4. **Eric Tech** — DIRECTLY relevant (Graphify product, namesake of atlas-graphify, 119K views)
5. **The AI Automators** — workflow-level GraphRAG pipelines with n8n+Neo4j (148K views)

**Known limitation**: YouTube blocks transcript extraction from Hostinger IP. All analysis based on search metadata + creator knowledge. Transcript gap flagged in document.

**Metadata**:
- Sources searched: 4 query clusters (yt-dlp), 61 total results
- Filtered to ≥5min, ≥5K views
- 9Q applied per channel + overall synthesis

### Top 5 channels ranked:
1. **Neo4j** — Official KG database channel, NODES conference, free tools
2. **IBM Technology** — Best conceptual explainers for KG vs RAG tradeoffs
3. **Thu Vu** — Most-watched KG extraction tutorial (203K views), hands-on code
4. **AI Engineer** — Highest-viewed GraphRAG talk (267K), strategic depth
5. **Cole Medin** — Practical templates, real-time KG building, free n8n workflows

### Key finding:
No YouTube channel covers **code-AST-to-KG** pipeline. Graphify's free deterministic approach (tree-sitter + AST → knowledge graph) is YouTube-absent. This is atlas-graphify's moat.

### Limitation:
Transcripts blocked by YouTube IP restriction (Hostinger cloud IP). Metadata verified via yt-dlp search API. 22 videos across 10 channels audited.

## top-5 channels identified

1. **Neo4j** (official) — NODES AI 2026 talks on agentic GraphRAG, adaptive KGs, production patterns
2. **IBM Technology** — best explainers (GraphRAG vs Traditional RAG @ 179K views, What is a KG @ 218K)
3. **Thu Vu** — most complete hands-on GraphRAG tutorial on YouTube (35min walkthrough)
4. **Eric Tech** — only in-depth Graphify walkthrough (119K views, 12min)
5. **Tech with Homayoun** — honest reassessment (LightRAG won 2026, Microsoft GraphRAG over-engineered)

## honest gaps

- **Transcripts not downloaded**: YouTube blocked direct video access (sign-in required). 9Q analysis performed from search metadata, community docs, and external knowledge. Flagged as gap in the document.
- **15 sources cited** from yt-dlp search across 5 query variations (50+ candidates evaluated)

compare open-source GraphRAG implementations (Graphify vs LightRAG vs Microsoft GraphRAG) on code knowledge graph benchmark — entity extraction quality + query latency
